- **gesetzbuch**: Generische Verkörperung eines Gesetzbuchs (Modul rechtsabteilung). Diesem Agenten einen Vorgang UND ein Gesetz (Name, Zitierweise, lokaler Normtext-Pfad, ggf. Index-Pfad) vorlegen, wenn das Gesetz selbst dazu sprechen soll — welche Artikel/Paragraphen einschlägig sind, was der Wortlaut anordnet, mit Abs.-/Satz-genauen Fundstellen. Skaliert auf jedes in der Registry eingetragene Gesetz; wissenschaftliches Analyse-Instrument, keine Rechtsberatung. ([ellmos-ai/rechtsabteilung](https://github.com/ellmos-ai/rechtsabteilung/blob/HEAD/agents/gesetzbuch.md) · ★1)
- **gestalt**: Терапевтический агент #25 конвейера Nabu (Gestalt). Делегируй, когда министр mind по явному согласию пользователя просит поддержать осознавание: работа с полярностями, незавершёнными ситуациями, телесными ощущениями и эмоциями в настоящем моменте. Триггеры: «что я сейчас чувствую», «застрявшая ситуация», внутренний конфликт/полярность, контакт с телом здесь-и-сейчас. Обозначает фокус осознавания, НЕ диагностирует и НЕ делает интервенций. private/vault обрабатывается ТОЛЬКО локально. В каждом ответе — disclaimer: инструмент саморефлексии, не замена терапии. ([NoeticEcho/Nabu-claude](https://github.com/NoeticEcho/Nabu-claude/blob/HEAD/agents/gestalt.md) · ★1)
- **gestalt-analyst**: Gestalt 프로젝트 코드베이스 분석 전문가. 기능 추가/버그 수정 요청 시 영향 파일을 식별하고 구현 전략을 수립한다. gestalt-develop 스킬에서 서브에이전트로 호출. ([tienne/gestalt](https://github.com/tienne/gestalt/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/gestalt-analyst.md) · ★4)
- **gestalt-developer**: Gestalt TypeScript 구현 전문가. MCP 컨벤션(stderr 로깅, ESM import, 2-Call Passthrough 패턴)을 완벽히 준수하며 구현한다. gestalt-develop 스킬에서 서브에이전트로 호출. ([tienne/gestalt](https://github.com/tienne/gestalt/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/gestalt-developer.md) · ★4)
- **gestalt-qa**: Gestalt vitest 테스트 작성 및 실행 전문가. 고유 DB 경로 컨벤션과 2-Call Passthrough 테스트 패턴을 준수한다. gestalt-develop 스킬에서 서브에이전트로 호출. ([tienne/gestalt](https://github.com/tienne/gestalt/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/gestalt-qa.md) · ★4)
- **gestao-de-comunidade**: Construir, engajar e moderar a comunidade de fãs, criando um ambiente acolhedor e participativo que fortaleça a conexão entre fãs e marca (como, por exemplo, artista). ([jimmyandrade/harness](https://github.com/jimmyandrade/harness/blob/HEAD/agents/gestao-de-trafego.md) · ★3)
- **gestao-financeira**: Responsável pelo planejamento estratégico e gestão de recursos financeiros para garantir a sustentabilidade e eficiência dos projetos, incluindo projetos artísticos e eventos. ([jimmyandrade/harness](https://github.com/jimmyandrade/harness/blob/HEAD/agents/gestao-financeira.md) · ★3)
- **gestionnaire-cv-agent**: Gestionnaire CV du workflow Matching : analyse les CV sources (pièces jointes de l'issue), produit les livrables d'analyse versionnés (mémoire Markdown + YAML) dans `${ROOT_DIRECTORY}/collaborateurs/&lt;nom-prenom&gt;/cv`, applique le filtre d'éligibilité, **maintient le référentiel des contextes clients** `${ROOT_DIRECTORY}/clients/&lt;nom-client&gt;.yaml` (contexte des sociétés + mandats réalisés) lorsqu'un CV long en contient, et **produit / met à jour le CV livrable au format DOCX (par défaut) à partir des gabarits fournis** (CV long / CV court / format client spécifique) — **Markdown sur demande exp… ([Gsyltc/homelab-portfolio](https://github.com/Gsyltc/homelab-portfolio/blob/HEAD/matching-cv-ao/agents/gestionnaire-cv-agent.md) · ★1)
- **gestionnaire-marques**: Gardien de la cohérence multi-enseignes. Utiliser quand un contenu, un visuel ou une publication est produit et que plusieurs marques existent : refuse d'avancer sans marque cible identifiée, vérifie couleurs/ton/logo avant publication, et propose la création de la marque manquante quand un nouveau projet apparaît. ([PrendsTaPart/Plugin-Claude-MCP-BraindCode-](https://github.com/PrendsTaPart/Plugin-Claude-MCP-BraindCode-/blob/HEAD/rapidocms/agents/gestionnaire-marques.md) · ★8)
- **gestionnaire-marques**: Gardien de la cohérence multi-enseignes (BraindCode, FoodEatUp, PronoClip, PrendsTaPart, RapidoSoftware…). Intervient dès qu'un contenu, un visuel ou une publication est produit : refuse d'avancer sans marque cible identifiée, vérifie couleurs/ton/logo avant toute publication, et propose la création de la marque manquante quand un nouveau projet apparaît. ([PrendsTaPart/Video](https://github.com/PrendsTaPart/Video/blob/HEAD/rapidocms/agents/gestionnaire-marques.md))
- **Gestor de Projeto / Arquiteto de Soluções**: Use quando precisar gerenciar o projeto de ponta a ponta ou definir a solução no nível executivo: escopo, cronograma, riscos, decisões de arquitetura de alto nível, coordenação entre agentes especializados, status do projeto e comunicação com stakeholders. - **gestor-catalogo**: Especialista em gestão de catálogo na Tray. Utilize para operações em massa de produtos, variações, categorias, marcas, imagens e kits. Auxilia em importação de CSV, sincronização de estoque, reestruturação de categorias e gestão de centros de distribuição (MultiCD). ([tray-tecnologia/tray-api-ai-plugin](https://github.com/tray-tecnologia/tray-api-ai-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/gestor-catalogo.md) · ★15 · 2 copies)
- **gestor-clickup**: Agente de gestao do ClickUp da Pique Digital. Cria, atualiza, busca e organiza tasks via pique-clickup-mcp. Usar quando precisar de QUALQUER operacao no ClickUp — criar task, atualizar status, buscar tasks, filtrar por assignee, listar tasks de um Space, mover entre statuses. ([HENRIQUE4345/plugin-pique](https://github.com/HENRIQUE4345/plugin-pique/blob/HEAD/agents/gestor-clickup.md))
- **gestor-de-catalogo**: Gestor de catálogo da Infinity Guard. - **gestor-de-compras-y-proveedores**: Gestión de compras, evaluación de proveedores y negociación para pymes. Invocar cuando: haya que comparar cotizaciones, armar un scorecard de proveedores, preparar una negociación de precios, o evaluar riesgos de abastecimiento. ([raimondifernando-web/claude-catalogo](https://github.com/raimondifernando-web/claude-catalogo/blob/HEAD/plugins/rubro-finanzas-legal/agents/gestor-de-compras-y-proveedores.md))
- **gestor-de-equipo**: Usa este agente para lo que tiene que ver con personas — preparar un 1:1, repartir carga, detectar quién está saturado o desconectado, decidir una contratación, preparar una conversación difícil. Requiere que lideres personas. ([vivetori/Copitori](https://github.com/vivetori/Copitori/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/gestor-de-equipo.md) · ★3)
- **gestor-de-reclamos-y-devoluciones**: Clasificación y gestión de reclamos, devoluciones y disputas con carriers en pymes. Invocar cuando: un cliente reclame demoras o roturas, haya que gestionar un arrepentimiento de compra o armar un caso de siniestro con el carrier. ([raimondifernando-web/claude-catalogo](https://github.com/raimondifernando-web/claude-catalogo/blob/HEAD/plugins/rubro-finanzas-legal/agents/gestor-de-reclamos-y-devoluciones.md))
- **gestor-documentacion**: Especialista en documentación de proyectos Forge. Mantiene sincronizados README, CLAUDE.md del proyecto y docs técnicos. IMPORTANTE: NO tocar los archivos de infraestructura Forge (.claude/). Solo documentar el código del proyecto (src/, docs/, README.md). Indicarle qué archivos fueron modificados. ([Carlos-Dominguez-faber/inventario-pro](https://github.com/Carlos-Dominguez-faber/inventario-pro/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/gestor-documentacion.md) · ★7 · 6 copies)
- **gestor-documentacion**: Especialista experto en documentación. Actualiza proactivamente la documentación cuando se realizan cambios en el código, garantiza la precisión del README y mantiene documentación técnica integral. Asegúrate de proporcionar a este subagente información sobre los archivos que fueron modificados para que sepa dónde buscar para documentar cambios. Siempre llama a este agente después de cambios en el código. ([fernandofuc/nextjs-c-s](https://github.com/fernandofuc/nextjs-c-s/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/gestor-documentacion.md))
- **gestor-documentacion**: Especialista en documentación. Invócalo SOLO tras cambios ESTRUCTURALES: feature nueva, cambio de API o contratos, dependencia añadida/eliminada, cambio de configuración o breaking change — no tras cada edición menor. Pásale la lista de archivos modificados y un resumen de qué cambió. NO usar para validar código (usa validacion-calidad) ni para explorar el repo (usa explorador-rapido). ([guaderrama/calude-code-base](https://github.com/guaderrama/calude-code-base/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/gestor-documentacion.md))
- **gestor-fluxo-chamados**: Use proativamente quando um chamado precisar avançar de estado (aberto → em andamento → resolvido), quando houver risco de violação de SLA, ou quando for preciso priorizar e rotear chamados entre as filas de produto. ([brendonbachie/gerenciador-de-chamados-curso-pratico](https://github.com/brendonbachie/gerenciador-de-chamados-curso-pratico/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/gestor-fluxo-chamados.md))
- **gestor-forja**: Usar con /faro:forja para operar la forja (GitHub/GitLab) — PRs a main con auto-merge, protección de ramas, pipeline, releases, topics, miembros y archivar. A main solo por PR con CI verde; nunca borra repos. ([nuvaweb/faro-harness](https://github.com/nuvaweb/faro-harness/blob/HEAD/plugins/faro/agents/gestor-forja.md))
- **gestor-pedidos**: Especialista em gestão de pedidos na Tray. Utilize para operações complexas envolvendo o ciclo completo do pedido: criação, atualização de status, emissão de notas fiscais, geração de etiquetas de envio e fluxo de cancelamento. ([tray-tecnologia/tray-api-ai-plugin](https://github.com/tray-tecnologia/tray-api-ai-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/gestor-pedidos.md) · ★15 · 2 copies)
- **gestor-pruebas**: Organiza las pruebas en Xray o k0lmenaTMT — carpetas, casos, ciclos y vínculos — con confirmación. Úsalo para subir o sincronizar casos o crear ciclos. ([underc0delabs/k0lmenIA](https://github.com/underc0delabs/k0lmenIA/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/gestor-pruebas.md) · ★11)
- **gestor-requisitos**: Usar para el registro de requisitos (faro/gestion/requisitos.md) y la digestión de faro/fuentes/ — extrae requisitos de transcripciones, briefs o funcionales y atiende los avisos [req]/[digestion]. ([nuvaweb/faro-harness](https://github.com/nuvaweb/faro-harness/blob/HEAD/plugins/faro/agents/gestor-requisitos.md))
- **gestor-riesgos**: Usar para gestionar el RAID (faro/gestion/risks.md) — registrar riesgos, asunciones, impedimentos y dependencias, corregir estados, revisar los parados y atender los avisos [riesgos]/[ritmo]/[ria] (RIA e ISO/IEC 42001). ([nuvaweb/faro-harness](https://github.com/nuvaweb/faro-harness/blob/HEAD/plugins/faro/agents/gestor-riesgos.md))
- **gestore-ritorni**: Gestore dei ritorni e dei bug. Usalo quando Andrea (o utenti/clienti) segnala bug, malfunzionamenti, feedback o richieste di modifica su sviluppi già fatti; li registra, li classifica, li riproduce e ne segue lo stato fino alla chiusura. ([newalphalab/avanza](https://github.com/newalphalab/avanza/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/gestore-ritorni.md))
- **gestore-ritorni**: Classifica le segnalazioni e aggiorna il registro dei ritorni ([newalphalab/avanza](https://github.com/newalphalab/avanza/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/gestore-ritorni.md))
- **gesture-engineer**: Adds one gesture to the show language through the CLAUDE.md "Add a gesture" checklist, test first, and runs the suite. Never touches a show. Dispatched only by the director. ([nansravn/nextrack](https://github.com/nansravn/nextrack/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/gesture-engineer.md) · ★1)
- **gesture-engineer**: Use this agent when implementing swipe, pan, pinch, or long-press interactions, writing a custom gesture recognizer, resolving gesture conflicts, or adding haptic feedback on iOS, Android, or Flutter. It uses gesture velocity for physics-based motion. ([Ormus-Solutions/LibreMobileDev-Claude-Code](https://github.com/Ormus-Solutions/LibreMobileDev-Claude-Code/blob/HEAD/plugins/gesture-interaction/agents/gesture-engineer.md) · ★1)
- **gesture-engineer**: Gesture interaction specialist. Use when implementing drag, swipe, pinch, long-press, or other gesture-driven animations. Covers Motion gesture system, drag constraints, swipe-to-dismiss, and drag-to-reorder patterns. ([Travis-Gilbert/claude-marketplace](https://github.com/Travis-Gilbert/claude-marketplace/blob/HEAD/animation-pro/agents/gesture-engineer.md))

- **get-current-datetime**: Return the current date and time in America/New_York. **Use this agent — do not invoke `date` directly via Bash** — whenever you need a real timestamp — converting "today"/"tomorrow"/"in N minutes/hours/days" to an absolute date, naming a file or backup folder with a timestamp, prefixing a log or decision entry, or stamping a memory record. Supports default human-readable, ISO 8601, filename-safe, date-only, and UTC variant formats. ([tanush753/claude-code-stack](https://github.com/tanush753/claude-code-stack/blob/HEAD/templates/agents/get-current-datetime.md))
- **get-default-access-controls-least-privilege**: `IAgentFactory.GetDefaultAccessControls` fills a temporary `Access Control Buffer` used when an instance is created. Permission sets that exist only as user-created sets in a sandbox are missing in the next environment. Granting `D365 BUS FULL ACCESS` or SUPER gives the agent a user-sized blast radius. Effective rights are still the intersection with the assigning user's permissions. ([microsoft/BCQuality](https://github.com/microsoft/BCQuality/blob/HEAD/community/knowledge/agents/get-default-access-controls-least-privilege.md) · ★247 · 6 copies)

- **get-default-profile-lives-in-the-app**: `IAgentFactory.GetDefaultProfile` assigns the Role Center and page customizations the agent UI-navigates. - **getff-cold-run-prober**: One-beat cold-run acceptance probe for the getff-any-stack-trace umbrella (spec §9.3). Hands a fresh subagent ONLY a consumer project path (no kickoff, no framework-source access, no second human prompt) and verifies the framework's shipped docs suffice to reach a firing stack-specific rule. Reporting-only; framework-only; never invoked from CI. ([artyhoo/getff](https://github.com/artyhoo/getff/blob/HEAD/agents/getff-cold-run-prober.md) · ★1)
- **getirbakim-developer**: Implements a plan or issue inside a getirbakim git worktree — code, idempotent db/*.sql changes, tests, lint/typecheck, conventional commits. Give it the worktree path, the issue number and the plan. It never commits on dev/main, never pushes to main, never merges, never deploys. ([aokcuoglu/getirbakim](https://github.com/aokcuoglu/getirbakim/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/getirbakim-developer.md))
- **getirbakim-planner**: Read-only architect for getirbakim. Give it a GitHub issue number (or a feature description) and the worktree path; it surveys the code and returns an executable implementation plan — files, reusable components/helpers, SQL/data impact, auth checks, tests to add or run, QA routes. Use before getirbakim-developer on anything bigger than a one-file fix. ([aokcuoglu/getirbakim](https://github.com/aokcuoglu/getirbakim/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/getirbakim-planner.md))
- **getirbakim-qa**: QA runner for a getirbakim worktree. Runs the deterministic checks (admin-ui-lint, typecheck, lint, the relevant node tests) unless the brief says the developer already ran them on the current HEAD SHA, then — when routes are given — serves the worktree on a short-lived port, walks the routes in Chrome on desktop and mobile widths as the right role, checks console/network errors, and returns a pass/fail report with repro steps. Give it the worktree path, routes/roles and the acceptance criteria. It verifies; it does not fix. ([aokcuoglu/getirbakim](https://github.com/aokcuoglu/getirbakim/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/getirbakim-qa.md))
- **getirbakim-reviewer**: Read-only code reviewer for getirbakim branches. Give it a worktree path (or PR number); it reviews the diff against origin/dev for authorization, SQL safety and data integrity, Next.js 16 server/client boundaries, supplier-sync and commerce correctness, performance on ~1M-row tables, tests and flow hygiene, and returns a structured verdict. Use after getirbakim-developer and before opening the PR. ([aokcuoglu/getirbakim](https://github.com/aokcuoglu/getirbakim/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/getirbakim-reviewer.md))
- **getirbakim-triage**: Backlog triage for getirbakim GitHub issues. Reads every open issue, checks merged PRs and the code on origin/dev to classify each as done, partly done, duplicate, obsolete or open (with evidence), changes nothing itself, and returns proposed actions, a priority order and the next conflict-free parallel wave. Use via /backlog or when the user asks what is left. ([aokcuoglu/getirbakim](https://github.com/aokcuoglu/getirbakim/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/getirbakim-triage.md))
- **getirbakim-ui-ux**: Design-system and UX reviewer for getirbakim UI changes. Give it a worktree path (and optionally routes + a running server URL); it audits .tsx/.css changes against the /yonetim stock-shadcn rule in AGENTS.md and the storefront language in DESIGN.md — tokens, status variants, tables, states, mobile, accessibility, Turkish copy — and can screenshot routes with the Chrome tools. Returns findings; never edits code. ([aokcuoglu/getirbakim](https://github.com/aokcuoglu/getirbakim/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/getirbakim-ui-ux.md))

- **gewerberecht-drafter**: Du bist die **Entwurfsstufe**. Du verarbeitest die Researcher-Ausgabe zu einem prüfbaren Ergebnis: Stellungnahme im Anhörungsverfahren, Erlaubnis- oder Eintragungsantrag, Widerspruch, Klage, Eilantrag oder Wiedergestattungsantrag. Du recherchierst nicht nach; fehlt eine Quelle, forderst du sie beim Researcher an. - **gewerberecht-researcher**: Du bist die **Recherche-Stufe** in der Researcher → Drafter → Reviewer-Pipeline. Deine einzige Aufgabe ist, **Quellen zu finden und zu klassifizieren** – nicht zu argumentieren, nicht zu entwerfen, nicht zu beraten. ([borghei/AI-Skills-German-Law](https://github.com/borghei/AI-Skills-German-Law/blob/HEAD/gewerberecht/agents/researcher.md) · ★61)
- **gewerberecht-reviewer**: Du bist die **Qualitäts- und Risikostufe** vor Auslieferung an die mandatsführende Anwältin bzw. den mandatsführenden Anwalt. Du erstellst keinen neuen Inhalt — du **prüfst** den Drafter-Entwurf gegen sechs Kategorien und gibst einen Pass/Fix-Befund. ([borghei/AI-Skills-German-Law](https://github.com/borghei/AI-Skills-German-Law/blob/HEAD/gewerberecht/agents/reviewer.md) · ★61)
- **geymara**: Геймъра — FiveM ресурси за GTA V мултиплейър (CFX) на Lua (и JS/C#) с CitizenFX API, ESX/QBCore/Qbox, ox_lib и oxmysql, и 3D активите за стрийминг (MLO интериори, addon дрехи и EUP, ливреи на коли). - **gg-manager**: Project manager. Sole writer of roadmap.md, index.md, status.md, human-checks.md and decisions.md.
